# Payload Byte Plots and Decode Audit

Generated from ROM bytes. Known sections are labeled; unknown/unresolved areas are explicitly marked.

ROM: /Users/v/Documents/GitHub/SoETilesViewer/SoEScriptDumper/Secret of Evermore (U) [!].smc

## Meaning of terms

- families: payload-declared tile family IDs. Byte 0 gives tileCount N, then N little-endian uint16 entries follow.
- sentinel type: the 7-byte boundary marker after a variable middle section. Observed signatures are 30 00 00 00 01 00 FF and C8 00 00 00 01 00 FF.
- nibble range: tilemap is nibble-packed, so every decoded tile index is always in 0..15.

## Name discrepancy to watch

- extension room list labels 0x5C as Raptors.
- one synthetic enum test mentions RAPTORS=0x38.
- both are audited below.
